### 1a — Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1
Status: **AGREE**

### 1e — Taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": false}, {"ref": "f2441.benefits"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"const": 0}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": true}, {"ref": "f2441.benefits"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"ref": "f2441.line26"}}], "op": "choose"}
```

Second:

```json
{"ref": "f2441.line26"}
```

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 24, Line 1e: "Enter the total of your taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26. Dependent care benefits should be shown in box 10 of your Form(s) W-2. But first complete Form 2441 to see if you can exclude part or all of the benefits"; Form 2441 p. 1, Part I: "Did you receive dependent care benefits? No. Complete only Part II below". The candidate copies f2441.line26 on every return; the project enters 0 unless f2441.benefits is yes, Part III then not being completed. executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 726 of 2,119 agree; 1,393 differ: 1,108 candidate-unknown and 276 candidate-refuses (completed returns: 448 candidate-unknown, e.g. classm.s8812.4.deepseek and classm.f5695.3.deepseek to .6.deepseek needing f2441.line14 from filers without benefits, project $0.00; 4 candidate-refuses), the rest on refused or incomplete returns (classm.f8889.4.deepseek, sa.excluded_income; classm.f4952.3.deepseek, f2441.student_disabled). Nine value differences (P $0.00, C $1,000.00; 7 on completed returns: stage18.blind.p6, .p10, .p20, stage18.mut.family_of_20, stage19.blind.e2, .e4, stage20.blind.r7; triage.py --explain 1e --witness 12) are records the interview cannot produce: each fixture answers f2441.benefits no and also carries a taxpayer W-2 box 10 of $1,000.00, which the interview never asks after that No (checked: stage18.blind.p6 evaluated with every box 10 answer removed leaves all 15 full-interview targets known and lines 1e, 1z and 34 unchanged), and the question itself names box 10 ("amounts shown in box 10 of a Form W-2"). The blind facts for those returns state no dependent care benefits, so the box 10 entries are stray fixture inputs, not evidence against the project's line. Unchanged by Stage 19; same ruling as round 4. Project stands.

### 4a — IRA distributions
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.amount_a", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"input": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}}
```

Second:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.box1", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"ref": "retirement.code"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": "roth_ira"}, {"ref": "retirement.account"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"ref": "retirement.rollover"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "any"}, {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"ref": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}], "test": "all"}}
```

Second transcription's note: A fully taxable IRA distribution is not entered on line 4a. The total distribution goes on 4a only when an exception applies. Exception 1 is a rollover. Exception 2 includes any Roth IRA distribution. Exceptions 3 and 4 (QCD, HFD) are screened per entry by retirement.special. A blank entry is represented as zero.

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 27, Exception 1, "Enter the total distribution on line 4a if you rolled over part or all of the distribution", and Exception 2 (Roth IRA distributions); p. 27, line 4b, "If the distribution from your IRA is fully taxable, enter the total distribution on line 4b; don't make an entry on line 4a". The candidate sums box 1 over IRA entries with a direct rollover (code G), a Roth IRA account or a rollover amount; the project sums retirement.amount_a, which is box 1 exactly when retirement.show_a (a rollover, a Roth account, or for plans an annuity with cost or box 5) and zero otherwise, so over IRA entries the two select the same exceptions; Exceptions 3 and 4 are screened per entry by retirement.special, as the candidate's note says. executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 2,084 of 2,119 agree; 35 differ, no value difference on any return: 34 project-refuses, all on refused returns, on the cited per-entry screens retirement.bad_code and retirement.special (classm.w2.1.deepseek, classm.otjob.1, classm.f8606.1), which are scope; 1 project-unknown on an incomplete partial record (stage10.ira.roth_code_unanswered, retirement.code withheld; the interview asks the code of every entry for those screens). Observed equivalence on the sample, not proof. Project stands.

### 4b — IRA distributions, taxable amount
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.taxable", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"input": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}}
```

Second:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.taxable", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"ref": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}}
```

Second transcription's note: If more than one distribution, the taxable amount of each is figured and the taxable amounts are totalled. retirement.taxable is read as each entry's own taxable amount.

Ruling: representation; executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 2,119 of 2,119 agree. Identical to the project's sum of retirement.taxable over non-plan (IRA) entries except that retirement.account is read as a reference instead of the input, the same per-entry answer. i1040gi p. 26, Lines 4a and 4b. Same ruling as round 4.

### 4c — Check if (box 1) Rollover
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"args": [{"const": 0, "type": "Count"}, {"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.one", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"input": "retirement.code"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"alternatives": [{"guard": {"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"input": "retirement.code"}], "test": "eq"}, "then": {"input": "retirement.box1"}}, {"guard": {"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"input": "retirement.code"}], "test": "ne"}, "then": {"input": "retirement.rollover"}}], "op": "choose"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "any"}, {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"input": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}], "test": "all"}}], "test": "lt"}
```

Second:

```json
{"args": [{"const": 0, "type": "Count"}, {"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.one", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"ref": "retirement.code"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"ref": "retirement.rollover"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "any"}, {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"ref": "retirement.account"}], "test": "ne"}], "test": "all"}}], "test": "lt"}
```

Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 27, Exception 1, "Also check box 1 on line 4c". Both check the box for an IRA entry with code G or a positive rollover amount; the project's second test reads box 1 for a code G entry, which its first test already covers, so the conditions are the same, and the candidate reads retirement.account as a reference. executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 2,085 of 2,119 agree; 34 differ, all project-refuses on refused returns (retirement.bad_code, retirement.special per entry: classm.w2.1.deepseek, classm.dcprov.1), which are scope; no value difference. Observed equivalence on the sample, not proof. Project stands.

### 5a — Pensions and annuities
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.amount_a", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"input": "retirement.account"}], "test": "eq"}}
```

Second:

```json
{"collection": "retirement", "field": "retirement.box1", "op": "sum_over", "where": {"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"args": [{"const": true}, {"ref": "retirement.annuity"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"ref": "retirement.cost"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "all"}, {"args": [{"const": "direct_rollover_G"}, {"ref": "retirement.code"}], "test": "eq"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"ref": "retirement.box5"}], "test": "lt"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"ref": "retirement.rollover"}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "any"}, {"args": [{"const": "plan"}, {"ref": "retirement.account"}], "test": "eq"}], "test": "all"}}
```

Second transcription's note: A fully taxable pension goes only on 5b. Box 1 goes on 5a when the payment is partially taxable or rolled over. Partially taxable means the Simplified Method applies with cost, or box 5 shows contributions. A blank entry is zero. An annuity whose cost was fully recovered before 2025 is a known simplification.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 28, Fully Taxable Pensions and Annuities: "Your payments are fully taxable if (a) you didn’t contribute to the cost (see Cost, later) of your pension or annuity, or (b) you got your entire cost back tax free before 2025. ... If your pension or annuity is fully taxable, enter the total pension or annuity payments (from Form(s) 1099-R, box 1) on line 5b; don’t make an entry on line 5a". The candidate enters box 1 on line 5a for any annuity with a cost, so an annuity whose cost was recovered before 2025 gets a line 5a amount (its note calls this "a known simplification"); the project tests retirement.annuity_with_cost, which needs cost left on the Simplified Method Worksheet (retirement.cost_left). executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 2,099 of 2,119 agree; 20 differ: 7 value differences, among them classm.tcw.15 (completed; annuity with cost, Simplified Method line 7 below zero, so no cost left: project $0.00, candidate $39,110.00; triage.py --explain 5a --witness 2) and stage10.smw.cost_recovered and .cost_recovered_box5 (incomplete; project $0.00, candidate $12,000.00); and 13 project-refuses on refused returns on cited screens (retirement.annuity_rollover, retirement.bad_code, retirement.special), which are scope. The value differences are the candidate's error. Project stands.

```json

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 31, Line 6c: "Check the box on line 6c if you elect to use the lump-sum election method for your benefits. If any of your benefits are taxable for 2025 and they include a lump-sum benefit payment that was for an earlier year, you may be able to reduce the taxable amount with the lump-sum election"; there is no election without benefits. The candidate checks the box from ssb.lump_sum alone; the project requires ssb.line1 above zero. executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 980 of 2,119 agree; 1,139 differ: 1,129 candidate-unknown (474 on completed returns, classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .6.deepseek, project unchecked: the election asked of filers with no benefits) and 10 value differences, all on refused returns (classm.eic.7, ssb.benefits $0 with ssb.lump_sum yes: project unchecked, candidate checked). Unchanged by Stage 19; same ruling as round 4. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: When the line 27c box is checked (the instructions said to check it, or the credit is not claimed), no EIC is entered. The closed interface has no input for an election not to claim an otherwise allowed EIC.

Ruling: model error; inherited from the candidate's lines 6b and 1e. The line itself (zero when the line 27c box is checked, else eic.credit, refusing on household.credit_disallowed) reads as the project's EIC; the filer's own choice not to claim is not asked by either (TASKS.md T-027). executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 1,145 of 2,119 agree; 974 differ: 815 candidate-unknown, 155 candidate-refuses, 4 project-refuses, no value difference; completed returns 460 candidate-unknown (classm.f2441.1.deepseek etc., ssb.pub915 through the candidate's line 6b, i1040gi p. 31) and 4 candidate-refuses (classm.s3.3.deepseek, project $120.00: f2441.plans_differ reached through eic.below_top, line 11b, line 1z and the candidate's line 1e = f2441.line26; triage.py --explain 27a --witness 9; i1040gi p. 24, Line 1e: "Enter the total of your taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26. Dependent care benefits should be shown in box 10 of your Form(s) W-2. But first complete Form 2441 to see if you can exclude part or all of the benefits"; Form 2441 p. 1, Part I: "Did you receive dependent care benefits? No. Complete only Part II below"). The 4 project refusals are cited screens (household.roster_bad, person.ira_dist_gate, household.filer_under16). Project stands. (The render review raises a separate defect of the EIC's own question eic.us_home, ruled there: finding H1.)

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 61, Line 29: "If you meet the requirements to claim an education credit (see the instructions for Schedule 3, line 3), enter on line 29 the amount, if any, from Form 8863, line 8", and p. 115, Line 3: "However, you can’t take an education credit if any of the following applies. • You, or your spouse if filing jointly, are claimed as a dependent on someone else’s (such as your parent’s) 2025 tax return"; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit, item 1. The project enters Form 8863 line 8 only when f8863.eligible (f8863.paid yes and sli.claimed no; married filing separately and nonresident aliens are refused for the whole return by s3.mfs and scope.other_income; the line 11b bar is Form 8863 line 4's stop) and zero otherwise; the candidate copies line 8 on every return, so Form 8863 is evaluated, with its refusals, for filers who paid no education expenses. executable-triage-v2 (triage/f1040.json; 1,306 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 313 partial records from 40 bases; 2,119 comparisons): 512 of 2,119 agree; 1,607 differ: 1,028 candidate-unknown, 578 candidate-refuses, 1 project-refuses, no value difference; completed returns 489 candidate-unknown and 4 candidate-refuses. On the completed witnesses (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .6.deepseek, f8863.paid no, project $0.00; triage.py --explain 29 --witness 0) the candidate's Form 8863 line 7 needs ssb.pub915 through line 3 = Form 1040 line 11b and the candidate's own line 6b, so the unknown is inherited; its own error is that Form 8863 is asked of every filer and its refusals (for example sa.excluded_income on line 3, classm.f8889.4.deepseek) reach filers who claim no credit, all on refused returns here. The one project refusal (generated 211, scope.schedule1 through Form 8863 line 3 = line 11b) is a return the project refuses on a cited screen: scope. The interface offers f8863.eligible: not an interface gap (p. 115 was not among the pages given, but p. 61 sends the reader to it and the published helper states the condition). New in Stage 19 (the line was a refused zero before). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: f1040.line1a
    printed: "1a"
    label: "Total amount from Form(s) W-2, box 1 (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.box1
    note: "The instructions say to enter the total of Form(s) W-2 box 1, including the spouse's on a joint return. This reads person.box1 as each person's total of box 1 over their Forms W-2. The tool has no other return-level aggregate of w2.box1."
  - id: f1040.line1e
    printed: "1e"
    label: "Taxable dependent care benefits from Form 2441, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: f2441.line26
  - id: f1040.line1z
    printed: "1z"
    label: "Add lines 1a through 1h"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line1a, f1040.line1e]
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "Lines 1b, 1c, 1d, 1f, 1g and 1h (household wages, unreported tips, Medicaid waiver payments, adoption benefits, Form 8919 wages and other earned income) are outside the tool and are screened by scope.other_income. Only lines 1a and 1e are added."
  - id: f1040.line2a
    printed: "2a"
    label: "Tax-exempt interest"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.tax_exempt_interest, div.box12]
    note: "The line 2a instructions say to also include exempt-interest dividends (Form 1099-DIV box 12). These are added from div.box12, because the supplied tax-exempt interest total excludes them."
  - id: f1040.line2b
    printed: "2b"
    label: "Taxable interest"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: sb.line4
    note: "Line 2b is the total taxable interest. Schedule B line 4 is read as that total whether or not Schedule B must be attached."
  - id: f1040.line3b
    printed: "3b"
    label: "Ordinary dividends"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: sb.line6
  - id: f1040.line4a
    printed: "4a"
    label: "IRA distributions"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.box1
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "roth_ira"}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [retirement.rollover, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.code, {const: "direct_rollover_G"}]}
    note: "A fully taxable IRA distribution is not entered on line 4a. The total distribution goes on 4a only when an exception applies. Exception 1 is a rollover. Exception 2 includes any Roth IRA distribution. Exceptions 3 and 4 (QCD, HFD) are screened per entry by retirement.special. A blank entry is represented as zero."
  - id: f1040.line4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "IRA distributions — Taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
    note: "If more than one distribution, the taxable amount of each is figured and the taxable amounts are totalled. retirement.taxable is read as each entry's own taxable amount."
  - id: f1040.h_ira_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of IRA distributions with a rollover (Exception 1)"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, ne: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, gt: [retirement.rollover, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.code, {const: "direct_rollover_G"}]}
  - id: f1040.line4c_rollover
    printed: "4c"
    label: "Check if (see instructions) 1 Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      gt: [f1040.h_ira_rollover_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]
  - id: f1040.line5a
    printed: "5a"
    label: "Pensions and annuities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.box1
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - op: test
                all:
                  - {op: test, eq: [retirement.annuity, {const: true}]}
                  - {op: test, gt: [retirement.cost, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [retirement.rollover, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.code, {const: "direct_rollover_G"}]}
              - {op: test, gt: [retirement.box5, {const: 0}]}
    note: "A fully taxable pension goes only on 5b. Box 1 goes on 5a when the payment is partially taxable or rolled over. Partially taxable means the Simplified Method applies with cost, or box 5 shows contributions. A blank entry is zero. An annuity whose cost was fully recovered before 2025 is a known simplification."
  - id: f1040.line5b
    printed: "5b"
    label: "Pensions and annuities — Taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.taxable
      where: {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
    note: "This totals the taxable part of each pension or annuity (Simplified Method Worksheet line 9, or the rollover computation). retirement.taxable is read as each entry's taxable amount."
  - id: f1040.h_plan_rollover_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of pension or annuity distributions with a rollover"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: retirement
      field: retirement.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [retirement.account, {const: "plan"}]}
          - op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, gt: [retirement.rollover, {const: 0}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [retirement.code, {const: "direct_rollover_G"}]}
  - id: f1040.line5c_rollover
    printed: "5c"
    label: "Check if (see instructions) 1 Rollover"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      gt: [f1040.h_plan_rollover_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]
  - id: f1040.line6a
    printed: "6a"
    label: "Social security benefits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: ssb.line1
  - id: f1040.line6b
    printed: "6b"
    label: "Social security benefits — Taxable amount"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: ssb.line18
    refuses: [ssb.pub915, ssb.lump_sum, ssb.terror_disability]
  - id: f1040.line6c
    printed: "6c"
    label: "If you elect to use the lump-sum election method, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: ssb.lump_sum
  - id: f1040.line6d
    printed: "6d"
    label: "If you are married filing separately and lived apart from your spouse the entire year, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [ssb.lived_apart, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line7a
    printed: "7a"
    label: "Capital gain or (loss). Attach Schedule D if required"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: sd.to1040
    refuses: [sd.other_forms]
  - id: f1040.line8
    printed: "8"
    label: "Additional income from Schedule 1, line 10"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s1.line10
  - id: f1040.h_qd_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: qualified dividends (line 3a) more than ordinary dividends (line 3b)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      gt: [f1040.line3a, f1040.line3b]
    note: "The line 3a instructions say qualified dividends are also included in the ordinary dividends on line 3b. So line 3a more than line 3b is inconsistent."
  - id: f1040.line9
    printed: "9"
    label: "Add lines 1z, 2b, 3b, 4b, 5b, 6b, 7a, and 8. This is your total income"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line1z, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, f1040.line4b, f1040.line5b, f1040.line6b, f1040.line7a, f1040.line8]
    refuses:
      - {when: f1040.h_qd_over, when_known: true}
  - id: f1040.line10
    printed: "10"
    label: "Adjustments to income from Schedule 1, line 26"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s1.line26
  - id: f1040.line11a
    printed: "11a"
    label: "Subtract line 10 from line 9. This is your adjusted gross income"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: subtract
      from: f1040.line9
      take: f1040.line10
  - id: f1040.line11b
    printed: "11b"
    label: "Amount from line 11a (adjusted gross income)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: f1040.line11a
  - id: f1040.line12e
    printed: "12e"
    label: "Standard deduction or itemized deductions (from Schedule A)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: sa.deduction
    refuses: [sa.dependent]
  - id: f1040.line13a
    printed: "13a"
    label: "Qualified business income deduction from Form 8995 or Form 8995-A"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: qbi.deduction
    refuses: [qbi.unsupported]
  - id: f1040.line13b
    printed: "13b"
    label: "Additional deductions from Schedule 1-A, line 38"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s1a.line38
  - id: f1040.line14
    printed: "14"
    label: "Add lines 12e, 13a, and 13b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line12e, f1040.line13a, f1040.line13b]
  - id: f1040.line15
    printed: "15"
    label: "Subtract line 14 from line 11b. If zero or less, enter -0-. This is your taxable income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: f1040.line11b
      take: f1040.line14
  - id: f1040.h_sd_gains
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing Schedule D and Schedule D lines 15 and 16 are both gains"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [sd.filed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [sd.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [sd.line16, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.h_sd_1819
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 18 or line 19 of Schedule D is more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [sd.line18, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [sd.line19, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.h_4952_4g
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You file Form 4952 and have an amount on line 4g"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [sd.filing4952, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f4952.line4g, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.h_use_sdtw
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Use the Schedule D Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, gt: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_sd_gains, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_sd_1819, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_4952_4g, {const: true}]}
    note: "The worksheet's exception says not to use either worksheet when Form 1040 line 15 is zero or less. That exception is applied here and in h_use_qdcg."
  - id: f1040.h_cgd_no_sd
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You don't have to file Schedule D and you reported capital gain distributions on line 7a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [sd.filed, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [f1040.line7a, {const: 0}]}
  - id: f1040.h_qdcg_trigger
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Qualified dividends on line 3a, capital gain distributions without Schedule D, or Schedule D lines 15 and 16 both more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [f1040.line3a, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_cgd_no_sd, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_sd_gains, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.h_use_qdcg
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Use the Qualified Dividends and Capital Gain Tax Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, gt: [f1040.line15, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          not: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_qdcg_trigger, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.h_use_regular
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Use the Tax Table or Tax Computation Worksheet"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      not:
        op: test
        any:
          - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_qdcg, {const: true}]}
  - id: f1040.line16
    printed: "16"
    label: "Tax (see instructions)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_sdtw, {const: true}]}
          then: sdtw.line47
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_qdcg, {const: true}]}
          then: qdcg.line25
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [f1040.h_use_regular, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: tax_on_amount, bind: {amount: f1040.line15, status: f1040.status}, line: tax}
    refuses: [scope.other_line16, sa.excluded_income]
    note: "The choice between the Tax Table and the Tax Computation Worksheet (under or over $100,000) is left to the tax_on_amount definition. The other line 16 taxes (Forms 8814 and 4972, section 962, education credit recapture, Forms 8621 and 8978) and Form 8615 are screened by scope.other_line16. The Foreign Earned Income Tax Worksheet is screened by sa.excluded_income."
  - id: f1040.line17
    printed: "17"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 2, line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s2.line3
  - id: f1040.line18
    printed: "18"
    label: "Add lines 16 and 17"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line16, f1040.line17]
  - id: f1040.line19
    printed: "19"
    label: "Child tax credit or credit for other dependents from Schedule 8812"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s8812.line14
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]
  - id: f1040.line20
    printed: "20"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 8"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s3.line8
  - id: f1040.line21
    printed: "21"
    label: "Add lines 19 and 20"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line19, f1040.line20]
  - id: f1040.line22
    printed: "22"
    label: "Subtract line 21 from line 18. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: f1040.line18
      take: f1040.line21
  - id: f1040.line23
    printed: "23"
    label: "Other taxes, including self-employment tax, from Schedule 2, line 21"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s2.line21
  - id: f1040.line24
    printed: "24"
    label: "Add lines 22 and 23. This is your total tax"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line22, f1040.line23]
  - id: f1040.line25a
    printed: "25a"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from Form(s) W-2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.box2
    note: "This reads person.box2 as each person's total of Form W-2 box 2 (federal income tax withheld), summed over both spouses."
  - id: f1040.line25c
    printed: "25c"
    label: "Federal income tax withheld from other forms (see instructions)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line25c_other, f8959.line24]
    note: "The line 25c instructions include Additional Medicare Tax withheld (Form 8959, line 24) with the W-2G, Schedule K-1, 1042-S, 8805 and 8288-A withholding."
  - id: f1040.line25d
    printed: "25d"
    label: "Add lines 25a through 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line25a, f1040.line25b, f1040.line25c]
  - id: f1040.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: ss.zero
  - id: f1040.line27a
    printed: "27a"
    label: "Earned income credit (EIC)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: true}]}
          then: f1040.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.check_27c, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.credit
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]
    note: "When the line 27c box is checked (the instructions said to check it, or the credit is not claimed), no EIC is entered. The closed interface has no input for an election not to claim an otherwise allowed EIC."
  - id: f1040.line27b
    printed: "27b"
    label: "Clergy filing Schedule SE (see instructions)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.clergy
  - id: f1040.line27c
    printed: "27c"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the EIC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.check_27c
  - id: f1040.separated_spouses
    printed: "Dependents (separated spouses)"
    label: "Check if your filing status is MFS or HOH and you lived apart from your spouse for the last 6 months of 2025, or you are legally separated according to your state law under a written separation agreement or a decree of separate maintenance and you did not live in the same household as your spouse at the end of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.separated_box
  - id: f1040.line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Additional child tax credit (ACTC) from Schedule 8812"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s8812.line27
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed]
  - id: f1040.line28_box
    printed: "28 (box)"
    label: "If you do not want to claim the ACTC, check here"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      eq: [{const: true}, {const: false}]
    note: "The closed input interface has no question for declining the ACTC. The box is therefore always unchecked (false)."
  - id: f1040.line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "American opportunity credit from Form 8863, line 8"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: f8863.line8
  - id: f1040.line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "Refundable adoption credit from Form 8839, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: param
      ref: ss.zero
    refuses: [scope.credits]
    note: "Form 8839 is outside the tool. Line 30 is zero when no other credit is claimed, and scope.credits refuses otherwise."
  - id: f1040.line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Amount from Schedule 3, line 15"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: s3.line15
  - id: f1040.line32
    printed: "32"
    label: "Add lines 27a, 28, 29, 30, and 31. These are your total other payments and refundable credits"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line27a, f1040.line28, f1040.line29, f1040.line30, f1040.line31]
  - id: f1040.line33
    printed: "33"
    label: "Add lines 25d, 26, and 32. These are your total payments"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line25d, f1040.line26, f1040.line32]
  - id: f1040.line34
    printed: "34"
    label: "If line 33 is more than line 24, subtract line 24 from line 33. This is the amount you overpaid"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: f1040.line33
      take: f1040.line24
  - id: f1040.h_alloc_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Amount refunded (line 35a) plus amount applied to 2026 estimated tax (line 36)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of: [f1040.line35a, f1040.line36]
  - id: f1040.h_alloc_over
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Consistency check: lines 35a and 36 together are more than line 34"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      gt: [f1040.h_alloc_total, f1040.line34]
    note: "Lines 35a and 36 are each described as an 'amount of line 34'. Their total cannot exceed line 34."
  - id: f1040.line37
    printed: "37"
    label: "Subtract line 33 from line 24. This is the amount you owe"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: f1040.line24
      take: f1040.line33
    refuses:
      - {when: f1040.h_alloc_over, when_known: true}
    note: "Line 37 applies only when line 24 exceeds line 33, so the result is floored at zero. The refund-allocation consistency check is attached here as the last line of the return, to avoid a cycle through line 34."
```
```
